# Review of the Blueprint–Lean alignment PRs #224–#233

Ten pull requests by littlebin530, opened on 5 November 2026, do for chapters 15–19 and appendices A–D and F what #210–#223 did for the earlier chapters (there is no PR for appendix E). Each rewrites the AI-written layer 2 of its chapter so that it follows the Lean formalisation (slopmaster2 `08fcdf1cca`), adds new clause proofs, and edits the `lean` and `uses` tags. This report describes every change and what it means for the blueprint.

It covers 630 files. Each of the 429 rewritten or new texts was read against the readable tex, its layer-2 clause and the cited Lean. The PRs were compared with `main` at their base (`13eeb096a`) and at today's head (`7fd36e75c`). The earlier review of #210–#212 is in `LEAN-ALIGNMENT-PR-REVIEW.md`.

## Summary

**The new proofs are mostly sound, but none of the ten PRs can be merged as it stands.**

What holds up:
- 239 of the 429 texts raise no issue and 119 raise only minor ones.
- The reviewers found no wrong mathematics in the new proofs. They follow the Lean proofs closely, often in more detail than the tex.
- As the descriptions say, the text of the human-written declarations is untouched.
- `blueprint check` passes on every PR head, and every link and formula resolves.

What stops a merge:
- **71 items drop what the declaration asserts.** A clause that stated the human declaration now states the narrower result that Lean happens to prove, and the original is not kept. Typical cuts: a general curve becomes A¹, a t-structure becomes one inequality, a scheme becomes a matrix group, an isomorphism becomes a cardinality, the Jacobian becomes Pic. Several are marked "bypassed by the Lean route" although Lean does prove the general statement.
- **All ten conflict with today's `main`** (6–31 files each, 185 in total). `main` has since merged #200, the declaration arrows from the Lean facts, and renumbered `order` after #200 moved clauses. The conflicts are in front matter (`order`, `uses`, some `lean`), plus 4 files where clause text conflicts.
- **The edits will be overwritten.**
  - The PRs edit the generated markdown, while `blueprint-layer-2.tex` is unchanged.
  - Since #238 (merged today), that tex has its own proof for every one of its 1,095 clauses.
  - The next `scripts/retex.py` run would put back the tex's clauses, replace the 197 clause proofs these PRs add with the tex's proofs, and delete the 5 new clauses.
- **#231 creates a dependency cycle** of 23 nodes, which survives a merge with `main`.
- **86 existing Lean names are dropped entirely.** These names still exist at the Lean revision the PRs cite. Many of them were matched by hand in #193's review of the appendices, or state the tex claim exactly, while the PR description calls them "absent".

| #233 | 2 | 5 | 2 | 1 | 6 | 2 |

The categories:
- *same*: reworded only.
- *clarified*: adds hypotheses or details implicit in the tex.
- *narrowed*: states less than the tex asserts.
- *broadened*: states more.
- *changed*: different content.
- *bypass*: says the tex assertion is "bypassed by the Lean route".

## Findings that need a decision

### Content dropped from layer 2 (71 major items)

In each item the clause used to restate (part of) the human declaration. It now states something narrower, or different, and nothing in layer 2 carries the declaration's claim any more. The parent declaration's own text is unchanged. Each item's full issue is in the tables below. The main ones per PR:

**#224, Pure sheaves on curves (6).**
- `theorem-weights-sums-squared-traces--1` now states only the real case.
- `proposition-weights-j-boundary-points--1` is cut to affine X₀, although Lean has the general theorem and the AI proof of `corollary-unramified-boundary-point--2` needs it.
- In `corollary-sums-squared-traces-j-shriek--1`/`--2` and `lemma-bounds-squared-traces--3`, the general curve becomes A¹ and ν(j_!G) = ν(j_*G) is dropped.
- In `lemma-growth-finite-corrections--1`, the equality criterion becomes a one-sided bound with a different hypothesis.

**#225, The Fourier transform (13).**
- In `proposition-fourier-transform-perverse-heart--1`/`--2`/`--3`, t-exactness keeps only the ᵖD^{≤n} half, and the equivalence of hearts is weakened.
- `proposition-fourier-transform-middle-extension--1` no longer states FT((j_*M)[1]) = (j'_*M')[1].
- In `lemma-perverse-sheaves-curve--1`/`--2`, `lemma-largest-punctual-subsheaf--1`/`--3` and `lemma-simple-objects--1`/`--2`, the curve C becomes A¹ and the t-structure and heart become a criterion.
- `proposition-fourier-inversion--1` reduces the rank-r bundle to A¹ over 𝔽_q.
- In `proposition-base-change-fourier-inversion--2`/`--3`, the projection formula and the stalk formula are narrowed.

**#226, Deligne–Laumon for curves (3).**
- `lemma-fourier-transform-boundary-sequence--2` drops "B′ has weights at most w" as "bypassed", but Lean proves it (`FourierCorrection.hasWeightsAtMost`), and the PR also removes that name from the parent.
- `lemma-twisted-envelope--1` is replaced by a different statement.
- `proposition-purity-lisse-part-fourier-transform--1` keeps only weight 0.

**#227, Deligne–Laumon in general (3).**
- `lemma-mixedness-descends-constant-extension--1` and its proof are replaced by an unrelated Frobenius-spanning lemma.
- `theorem-pure-lisse-sheaves-over-relative--1` no longer states "mixed of weights at most w+i".

**#228, Proof of the Weil conjectures (5).**
- `theorem-weil-conjectures-smooth-proper-schemes--text-1` makes X₀ geometrically connected for the whole theorem. It also builds P₀ = 1−T and P_{2d} = 1−q^dT into the definition of a Weil factorization, which is false in general (e.g. Spec 𝔽_{q²}).
- `--1` no longer states item (1) for general X₀.
- `--16` replaces the functional equation by a root-multiset identity; its own proof says it does not prove the equation.
- `lemma-hankel-rank-rationality--1` deletes the Hankel-rank condition.
- `corollary-purity-cohomology-smooth-proper-variety--1` is restricted to geometrically connected X₀.

**#229, Appendix A (12).**
- In Chow's lemma, `--1` is cut to an affine base, and `--2` drops the projectivity of π and fπ. The PR calls `chow_relative_ample` absent, but it exists and is removed.
- Zariski's main theorem (`--1`) is marked "bypassed" though Lean has it, and the PR removes that Lean name.
- `passing-schematic-closure--1`/`--2` lose the general-Λ statement.
- Other clauses are deleted or replaced:
  - `remark-base-change--1` and `remark-non-noetherian--1`;
  - `making-open-schematically-dense--1`;
  - `products-composites--1`;
  - `affine-covers--2`, which drops "projective";
  - `gluing--1`, which now needs Noetherian;
  - `compactifying-union--1`, which silently drops "M separated".

**#230, Appendix B (9).**
- `lemma-symmetric-ample-sheaf--1` keeps only quasi-projectivity, though `proposition-multiplication-n`'s proof cites it for projectivity.
- `proposition-multiplication-n--1`/`--2` drop "finite locally free of degree n^{2g}" and "[n] étale".
- The Tate-module clauses are restricted to algebraically closed k.
- `h-1-structure-sheaf--1` is replaced by a different statement.
- `theorem-cube--1` and `corollary-triviality--1` become the same statement with the same Lean tag, so neither the theorem of the cube nor the f_*L claim is stated.

**#231, Appendix C (11).**
- `proposition-h-1-curve-tate-module--1` replaces H¹(C, ℤ/ℓⁿ(1)) ≅ J₀[ℓⁿ] by a Kummer isomorphism with Pic. The Lean names that state the Jacobian version are removed as "unsupported".
- `theorem-picard-scheme-jacobian-over-separably--4` says the tangent-space theorem "occurs only in comments"; it is a real theorem (`Picard/Jacobian.lean`).
- `corollary-degree-jacobian--1` no longer says ker(deg) = J(C)(k).
- `lemma-representability-picard-functor--1` is now only a general representability criterion.
- In `corollary-torsion-jacobian-field-extension--2`/`--4`, J and the field-extension invariance needed by proper base change are lost.
- Also affected:
  - `corollary-serre-duality-field-extension--1`
  - `lemma-coherent-base-change-invertible-sheaves--2`
  - `lemma-twisting-h-0-1-h--1`/`--2`

**#232, Appendix D (5).**
- `proof/proposition-cohomology-smooth-affine-curve--2` is circular: it cites `theorem-cohomology-curves--2`, which the notes prove from this proposition.
- `proposition-cohomology-smooth-affine-curve--3` keeps only a cardinality, so freeness is lost.
- `lemma-normalization-constant-sheaf--1` loses the branch formula.
- `proposition-p-torsion-smooth-proper-curve--2`/`--4` lose the link to H¹(C, O_C) and the bound "dimension ≤ g".

**#233, Appendix F (4).**
- The closed-image, Lie-criterion and Cartier clauses are cut to GL_n or a proper source:
  - `lemma-connected-components-images-homomorphisms--2`/`--3`;
  - `proposition-cartier-s-theorem--2`/`--3`.
- The general Lean theorems exist (`isClosed_range_of_isMonHom`, `GrpObj.isIso_left_of_differential_bijective`, `GrpObj.finrank_lie_eq_topologicalKrullDim`, `GrpObj.lieEquivIdentityComponent`), and the PR removes them.

### Conflicts with `main`

Since the PRs' base (`13eeb096a`, this morning), `main` has merged 34 commits. Three of them matter here:
- **#200** recomputed the declaration arrows from the Lean facts, moved eight clauses to break cycles, and changed some Lean names.
- **#206/#207** added the parts, which renumbered `order`.
- **#238** gave every clause of `blueprint-layer-2.tex` its own proof.

A test merge of each PR into `main` (`7fd36e75c`) gives:

| PR | Conflicting files | touching `order` | `uses` | `lean` | clause text |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| #224 | 27 | 23 | 27 | 8 | 0 |
| #225 | 31 | 31 | 27 | 13 | 0 |
| #226 | 12 | 8 | 12 | 1 | 0 |
| #227 | 18 | 12 | 18 | 2 | 0 |
| #228 | 6 | 6 | 6 | 3 | 0 |
| #229 | 31 | 27 | 23 | 14 | 2 |
| #230 | 12 | 12 | 9 | 5 | 0 |
| #231 | 25 | 23 | 19 | 10 | 1 |
| #232 | 16 | 16 | 14 | 6 | 0 |
| #233 | 7 | 5 | 5 | 2 | 1 |

Resolving the `uses` conflicts means choosing between #200's arrows and the PR's. The PRs' arrows also follow Lean dependencies, but were computed independently of #200.

### Dependency cycle (#231)

#231 creates a strongly connected component of 23 nodes, for example:

`corollary-degree-jacobian` → `theorem-picard-scheme-jacobian-over-separably--3` → `--1` → `lemma-coherent-base-change-invertible-sheaves` → `lemma-perfect-complexes-geometric-point` → `corollary-degree-jacobian`

- The arrow that closes it is `lemma-coherent-base-change-invertible-sheaves(--1)` → `lemma-perfect-complexes-geometric-point`. It is not a real mathematical dependency. The two only share the Lean lemma `DerivedCategory.isPerfect_of_finiteTorAmplitude`, a fact about modules over a ring.
- The arrow `lemma-perfect-complexes-geometric-point` → `corollary-degree-jacobian` is on `main` too, so the cycle survives a merge.
- The fix is to drop the arrow to `lemma-perfect-complexes-geometric-point` from the declaration and from `--1`'s proof.

No other PR creates a cycle on its own head.

### How the PRs are made

- **Generated files edited, tex untouched.**
  - All 429 texts exist only in `blueprint/`.
  - Among them are 5 new clauses with no `bp-r…` label in `blueprint-layer-2.tex`:
    - `theorem-chow-s-lemma-projective-form--3`
    - `lemma-cohomology-multiplicative-group--7`, `--8`
    - `lemma-p-torsion-affine-curve-characteristic--3`, `--4`
  - Since #238, the tex already has a proof for each clause, so the 197 clause proofs these PRs add duplicate work now on `main`. A retex run would replace them.
- **Lean names.**
  - Added: 359 tags, 298 distinct names. A source scan at `08fcdf1cca` finds 292 of them, and the reviewers confirmed the rest.
    - `IsWeilFactorization.apply_zero`, `.apply_last` and `.factorization` (#228) are structure fields. Tagging them makes some clauses true by definition.
    - `instGrpObjOverSchemeSpecOfIdentityComponent` (#233) is an auto-generated instance name, which is fragile as a tag.
    - `IsConnectedCurve.sheafH_constantZMod_of_isAffine` (#232) is a real theorem.
  - One name, `isIntegral_inv_of_mem_roots_map_intCast` (#228, on `theorem-weil-conjectures-smooth-proper-schemes--4`), exists only after the pinned Lean revision `f3cad3b`. `check --lean` fails until the pin moves.
  - Removed: 109 tags. 86 names are dropped from their PR entirely although they still exist at `08fcdf1cca`.
    - The PRs keep only names reachable from Lean's main theorem.
    - The appendix PRs (#229–#233) undo much of #193's hand review. Examples: `chow_relative_ample`, `dense_range_j_of_qcqs`, `etale_npow_id`, `JacobianTorsionDatum.kummerEquiv`, `nonempty_tangentSpaceAt_linearEquiv_H_one`, `IsCurve.sheafH_gmSheaf_of_smooth`, `GrpObj.lieEquivIdentityComponent`.
    - Some parent declarations are left with `lean = []`.
- **Arrows.** The PRs add 1,215 `uses` arrows and remove 503.
  - 396 of the added arrows point to definitions and 302 to layer-2 clauses.
  - About 180 go from a proof to an item later in the order.
- **"Bypass" claims.** 50 texts carry a bypass notice.
  - It is harmless when the tex statement is kept below it.
  - Many delete the statement instead.
  - Several claim Lean lacks a statement that Lean has (#226, #229, #231, #232, #233).
- **Lean leaking into the text.** Proofs narrate Lean internals (private helpers, file paths, "mainline", "wrapper"), and statements copy Lean conventions (finrank = 0 in infinite dimension, universe hypotheses, "trace datum", "integral realization").

### Patterns rated minor

- **Narrowed to Lean's intermediate theorem.** Clauses that repeated their parent now state a step Lean proves: étale instead of pro-étale, Weil sheaves instead of complexes, geometrically connected X₀, an algebraically closed field. The parent still makes the full claim.
- **Notation clashes.** Examples: `g` naming a Picard class, `P` both the Picard scheme and a point, `p` both a characteristic and a map. Also, `lemma-artin-schreier-character-sheaf--4`/`--5` refer to a trivialization that is no longer defined.
- **Hypotheses made explicit.** Implicit hypotheses are now explicit, usually correctly, and many "clarified" and "broadened" clauses are improvements worth keeping:
  - the schematic closure, uniqueness, Nagata and separation clauses in #229;
  - the Lie-algebra clauses in #233;
  - the four step-clauses added in #232.

## How this was checked

- **The change data.** Each PR's head was fetched, and every changed file was compared with the base `13eeb096a`: whether its text changed, which `lean` and `uses` entries were added or removed, and which other attributes changed.
- **The human-written text.** Checked mechanically: no declaration's text changed.
- **Tool checks.** `blueprint check` (tool `17f7e02`, as pinned at the base) ran on every PR head. Each PR was test-merged into today's `main` (`7fd36e75c`), and the conflicting lines were classified.
- **Cycles.** The `uses` graph (statements and proofs) was searched for strongly connected components on every PR head, on the base, and on `main`.
- **The texts.** Each rewritten or new text was read by one of five reviewers against the readable tex, the layer-2 tex (both at the base) and the Lean source at `08fcdf1cca`, and classified with a severity. One major finding per PR group was spot-checked by hand.
- **Lean names.** Checked by scanning declaration headers in the weil-conjectures sources at `08fcdf1cca` and at the pinned `f3cad3b`, plus the pinned Mathlib and proetale packages, and by the reviewers for the names the scan missed. This is not a Lean build.
